## Selecting candidates

Use the [Stellaris 4.5.4 deposit table](../../TMP/PDE/v4.5.4/DEPOSITS_RU.csv) to find candidates.

For each approved deposit, the [PDE manual placement rules](REQUIREMENTS_EN.md#manual-placement-rules) determine availability on the selected object.

- For orbital deposits, look for a positive number in a resource column. It gives the direct output in the specified mode. The Russian label “Условный Бонус” denotes an indirect effect.
- For planetary deposits, look for a direct increase in district capacity. Check whether the increase applies to the planet itself or to a habitat in the system.
- For event deposits with multiple sources, an anomaly takes precedence over an origin. Record the origin and other confirmed sources in the same entry.
- An orbital resource deposit found through a regular survey anomaly can be classified under Orbital deposits. Living metal from the Peculiar Crater anomaly is one example.

## Placement by ID

For a generic colonizable-world requirement, `cdpd_is_colonizable_or_colonized` tests `is_colonizable = yes` or `is_colony = yes`. Orbital resource targets must fail both tests. Individual class and climate restrictions still apply.

The **Where to add** column records the physical target for each ID’s add button. Listed classes are alternatives. “Colonizable world” refers to the type of body regardless of settlement. “Natural” additionally requires `is_artificial = no`. Access to the decision is checked separately.Players can repeatedly place every eligible ID on a matching target. The player’s empire type does not change the list. Arkship features require the corresponding game object. The two astral rift deposits are hidden from planetary-decision placement. Every entry under Orbital also requires an uncolonizable body. Removal checks only for an existing instance. [PDE requirements](REQUIREMENTS_EN.md#manual-placement-rules)

For regular generation the column gives the physical filter in the deposit definition. Weights that increase the chance in a particular climate leave the other eligible classes available. For scripted placement the target is evaluated at the point the deposit is granted. Nebulae, the identity of a named world or system, and the owner’s origin do not gate placement. Runtime class selection is shown as its original predicate or selection method. The adjacent columns describe output and quest rewards. [Planet classes](../../TMP/PDE/v4.5.4/planet_classes.json) · [Deposit definitions](../../TMP/PDE/v4.5.4/deposits.json)

For manual placement all amounts in a basic orbital series share one eligible body list. Each resource has its own list. Use the regular vanilla resource filter. For physics and engineering research use the full `can_have_physics_deposits`, `can_have_engineering_deposits` filters for every amount. Grants from anomalies, events, rifts and named systems are recorded solely as vanilla sources. This rule covers the 13 basic series for which vanilla defines a resource filter.

For former Remnants colonies PDE uses `is_starting_planet_type`, covering the nine ordinary climate classes (`pc_continental`, `pc_ocean`, `pc_tropical`, `pc_arid`, `pc_desert`, `pc_savannah`, `pc_alpine`, `pc_arctic`, `pc_tundra`) and `pc_volcanic`. The vanilla event selects a guaranteed neighbour by the `prescripted_ideal` flag. Each entry also includes eligible targets from its other sources. [Starting classes](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/scripted_triggers/00_scripted_triggers.txt:564) · [Neighbour class and flag](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/solar_system_initializers/empire_initializers.txt:863) · [Six deposits](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/origin_events_3.txt:249)

### Earth features

The nine Earth features and two Earth blockers share one class list. PDE takes the union of placement routes. The target can be in any system regardless of the empire’s origin or its current species preference.

| Placement route | Target classes |
| --- | --- |
| Earth in the fixed Sol initializers | `pc_continental`. [Earth](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/solar_system_initializers/sol_initializers.txt:88) · [Earth feature selection at game start](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/game_start.txt:1755) · [Earth feature set](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/scripted_effects/01_start_of_game_effects.txt:1486) |
| Natural starting classes and species-preferred class | `pc_continental`, `pc_ocean`, `pc_tropical`, `pc_desert`, `pc_arid`, `pc_savannah`, `pc_arctic`, `pc_alpine`, `pc_tundra`, `pc_volcanic`. [Starting classes](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/planet_classes/00_planet_classes.txt:73) · [Volcanic world](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/planet_classes/00_planet_classes.txt:430) |
| Starting-colony class override retaining the Earth setup | `pc_gaia`, `pc_nuked`, `pc_machine`. [Gaia world](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/governments/civics/00_origins.txt:2333) · [Tomb world](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/governments/civics/00_origins.txt:2405) · [Machine world](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/governments/civics/00_origins.txt:2537) |

In the default branch of `colony.9850` the copy spawns as `pc_ocean`. Then `change_pc = root.owner_species` changes its class before the Earth generator runs. Ocean is in the union when it is the final class. The separate Ocean Paradise, Life-Seeded and post-apocalyptic branches call the generic starting setup. The script does not enumerate unusual acquired species preferences. Adding a further special class requires evidence that it can be the final class. [Event branches](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/colony_events_3.txt:8228) · [Class change and placement](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/colony_events_3.txt:8349)

`d_delhi_sprawl` and `d_pacific_algae_tracts` inherit their Earth blockers’ list. Vanilla reveals each on the same body via `blocker_swap_types`. Generic starting blockers and ordinary district features that can also appear on Earth keep their own placement sources and constraints. [Pacific blocker](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/05_starting_deposits.txt:135) · [Earth infrastructure](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/05_starting_deposits.txt:162)

## PDE menu exclusions

These exclusions apply to manual placement. Each listed ID is eligible for removal when the deposit is present on the selected body. [Remove deposits](REQUIREMENTS_EN.md#remove-deposits)

**Astral rifts.** `d_astral_threads_deposit_2` and `d_crystal_rift` retain their entries in the category tables. Vanilla places both on the rift itself. The planetary decision hides placement on every body, including astral scars.

**Subterranean civilization markers.** Teeming Farming Caverns (`d_underground_farm_blocked`), Teeming Generator Areas (`d_underground_generator_blocked`) and Teeming Mining Sites (`d_underground_mine_blocked`) have empty modifiers. They occupy no districts and have no normal clearing action. The opening event creates the subterranean civilization and starts the contact chain separately. Destroying the civilization replaces all three markers with clearable `*_dead` blockers. [Definitions](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/02_event_planetary_deposits.txt:785) · [First contact](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/colony_events_1.txt:614) · [Marker replacement](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/colony_events_1.txt:705)

**Splinter Hive Symbiotic Link** (`d_splinter_hive_link`). The Splinter Hive holding places the spawning complex on the subject’s planet. On the overlord’s capital, it adds this deposit and increments the link counter. Demolishing the holding removes the link and decrements the counter. Manual placement would give hive species +2 pop growth independently of the holding and its counter. [Definition](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/08_overlord_deposits.txt:203) · [Growth bonus](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/scripted_variables/04_scripted_variables_overlord.txt:21) · [Construction and demolition](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/buildings/15_overlord_holdings.txt:600) · [Link counter](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/scripted_effects/overlord_effects.txt:830)

**Mountainous Vault** (`d_ancient_manufactorium_vaultexploration`). Temporary exploration marker in the Hidden Vault chain. It has no jobs, output or modifiers. The next event is queued separately, so manual placement only adds an entry to the planetary features list. [Definition](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/02_event_planetary_deposits.txt:1946) · [Placement and follow-up](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/colony_events_3.txt:5233)

**Central Spire** (`d_central_spire`). The old definition grants +15% to all three research fields from jobs and 400 jobs in each field or their gestalt equivalents. In 4.5.4 clearing the collapsed spire installs the urban zone `zone_central_spire`. The former blocker swap is commented out. The reviewed scripts contain no active placement for the deposit. [Definition](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/06_ancient_relics_deposits.txt:209) · [Spire clearing](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/06_ancient_relics_deposits.txt:20)

**Memorial to the Unshackled** (`d_crashed_slaver_ship_memorial`). The deposit adds 0.5 unity from civilians. The current Broken Shackles decision removes the crashed ship and creates the `zone_broken_shackles_memorial` zone. Building conditions still check for the deposit. The reviewed scripts contain no active placement for it. [Definition](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/09_first_contact_deposits.txt:20) · [Ship removal](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/decisions/08_first_contact_dlc_decisions.txt:29) · [Zone creation](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/decisions/08_first_contact_dlc_decisions.txt:76) · [Building condition](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/buildings/16_first_contact_buildings.txt:97)

**Wandering Forests** (`d_wandering_forests`). Random generation is disabled. The 4.5.4 scripts checked only remove this blocker. The Migrating Forests chain places a separate feature, `d_migrating_forests`. [Definition](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/01_blocker_deposits.txt:841) · [Removal](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/scripted_effects/00_scripted_effects.txt:8176) · [Colony event](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/colony_events_1.txt:57)

**Wilderness industrial ruins** (`d_wilderness_industrial`, `d_wilderness_industrial_2`). Both placement commands are commented out in the ownership transfer event. Random generation is disabled and no other source was found in the 4.5.4 scripts. If placed manually, Metallic Mire would occupy two district slots and Biochemical Badlands four. Clearing takes 120 days and costs 300 or 500 energy credits respectively. [Disabled branch](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/origin_events_wilderness.txt:221) · [Base variant](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/12_wilderness_deposits.txt:108) · [Larger variant](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/12_wilderness_deposits.txt:236)

**Shimmering Structure** (`d_shimmering_structure`). The blocker reduces the total district cap by 1 and cannot be cleared normally. The colony event places it and separately schedules the next event. Placing the blocker through PDE would leave the chain inactive. Both story outcomes remove it. [Clearing restriction](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/15_strange_worlds_deposits.txt:18) · [Event chain start](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/strange_worlds_events.txt:298) · [First outcome](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/strange_worlds_events.txt:379) · [Second outcome](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/strange_worlds_events.txt:444)

**Broken Shackles Ship Debris** (`d_ship_debris_broken_shackles_blocker`). The homeworld starts with five blockers, each occupying one district slot. Normal clearing is disabled. The origin creates a separate dig site, and each of its five stages removes one blocker. Placing the debris through PDE would not create the dig site. [Clearing restriction](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/09_first_contact_deposits.txt:33) · [Starting debris](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/inline_scripts/game_start/origin_broken_shackles.txt:44) · [Dig site creation](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/origin_events_1.txt:3078) · [Debris removal](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/origin_events_broken_shackles.txt:1488)

**Decrepit Tunnels without a placement source.** `d_decrepit_tunnels_3` is excluded from the manual menu. Stellaris 4.5.4 disables random generation for this variant. The advanced empire setup attempts to clear an existing instance on the ring. No placement command was found in the vanilla scripts. Its effects for the player match the two variants the game places. [Definition](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/07_federations_deposits.txt:288) · [Advanced start check](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/scripted_effects/01_start_of_game_effects.txt:3323)

**Archaeology event blockers.** Archaeological Site (`d_archaeological_site`) is excluded from manual placement. In Hidden Worlds and The Sentinels, the event creates a dig site and places the blocker with separate commands. Manual placement reduces total district capacity by 1. Normal clearing is disabled, and the event chain removes the blocker. [Definition](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/01_blocker_deposits.txt:1460) · [Hidden Worlds](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/ancient_relics_arcsite_events_1.txt:5015) · [The Sentinels](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/ancient_relics_arcsite_events_1.txt:5746) · [Removal](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/ancient_relics_arcsite_events_1.txt:5869)

**Arc Furnace** — `d_arc_furnace_1`, `d_arc_furnace_2`, `d_arc_furnace_3` and `d_arc_furnace_4`. All four IDs are excluded from manual placement through the PDE menu. Each has zero random generation weight. Building the furnace places the first-stage deposit on eligible bodies in the system. Each upgrade replaces the previous stage’s deposit, and restoring a ruined furnace places the fourth-stage deposit directly. Repeated rows for stages three and four in the source table represent different output modes of the same deposit ID. [Deposit definitions](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/13_machine_age_deposits.txt:558) · [Megastructure stages](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/megastructures/17_orbital_arc_furnace.txt:325) · [Upgrade replacement](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/machine_age_events_2.txt:61) · [Furnace restoration](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/machine_age_events_2.txt:159)

After anomaly research, the game can reapply the deposit only if the body already has an Arc Furnace modifier. The Asteroid Triplets anomaly also checks for a furnace in the system before applying a modifier. [Modifier check](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/machine_age_events_2.txt:177) · [Megastructure check](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/anomaly_events_cosmic_storms.txt:247)

**Consumer Goods Deposit** (`d_hab_consumer_goods_2`) is excluded from manual placement. This habitat feature produces 2 consumer goods per month. A script checks the source body for a Spire of Commodities (`d_consumer_goods_obsessional_directive`), adds the feature to the habitat and tags it with the source body. No active construction path to this effect was found in the vanilla Stellaris 4.5.4 habitat or orbital scripts. Its random generation weight is zero. [Definition](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/01_planetary_deposits.txt:2732) · [Source body](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/scripted_effects/federations_event_effects.txt:2519) · [Habitat construction](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/megastructures/habitats.txt:249) · [Major orbital](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/megastructures/habitats.txt:445) · [Minor orbital](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/megastructures/habitats.txt:493)

**Strategic resources on habitats.** All 15 IDs in these three series are excluded from the manual menu. With the required technology, denominations 1 through 5 grant 100 through 500 extraction jobs or their drone equivalents. Staffed jobs produce the resource. Random placement weight is zero.

- Rare crystals — `d_hab_crystal_1`, `d_hab_crystal_2`, `d_hab_crystal_3`, `d_hab_crystal_4`, `d_hab_crystal_5`. Rare Crystal Mining unlocks jobs for crystal miners. In Ithome’s Gate the research habitat receives the `_2` variant. [Definitions](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/01_planetary_deposits.txt:1908) · [Preset habitat](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/solar_system_initializers/special_system_initializers.txt:3137)
- Exotic gases — `d_hab_gas_1`, `d_hab_gas_2`, `d_hab_gas_3`, `d_hab_gas_4`, `d_hab_gas_5`. Exotic Gas Extraction unlocks jobs for gas extractors. In Ithome’s Gate the energy habitat receives the `_2` variant. [Definitions](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/01_planetary_deposits.txt:1703) · [Preset habitat](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/solar_system_initializers/special_system_initializers.txt:3165)
- Volatile motes — `d_hab_mote_1`, `d_hab_mote_2`, `d_hab_mote_3`, `d_hab_mote_4`, `d_hab_mote_5`. Mote Stabilization unlocks jobs for mote harvesters. In Ithome’s Gate the mining habitat receives the `_2` variant. [Definitions](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/01_planetary_deposits.txt:2113) · [Preset habitat](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/solar_system_initializers/special_system_initializers.txt:3151)

The script that copies a deposit from the source body still exists. No call was found in the current standard construction handlers for the central complex, major orbital or minor orbital. To qualify for resource buildings and specializations, a habitat checks its system for a mining-station deposit of the relevant resource. [Copy effect](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/scripted_effects/federations_event_effects.txt:2145) · [Central complex](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/megastructures/habitats.txt:249) · [Major orbital](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/megastructures/habitats.txt:445) · [Minor orbital](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/megastructures/habitats.txt:493) · [Resource checks](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/scripted_triggers/01_scripted_triggers_buildings.txt:1167)

**Habitat alloy deposits.** The entire `d_hab_alloy_*` series is excluded from every PDE menu category. Stellaris 4.5.4 defines `d_hab_alloy_1`, `d_hab_alloy_2`, `d_hab_alloy_3`, `d_hab_alloy_4`, `d_hab_alloy_5`, `d_hab_alloy_10` and `d_hab_alloy_25`. The exclusion includes the +4 variant placed on two habitats in Ithome’s Gate. [Definitions](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/01_planetary_deposits.txt:2578) · [Mining habitat](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/solar_system_initializers/special_system_initializers.txt:3150) · [Generator habitat](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/solar_system_initializers/special_system_initializers.txt:3164)

**Living Metal Deposit on a habitat** (`d_hab_living_metal_1`) is excluded from the PDE manual menu. It produces 1 living metal per month if the owner has the Living Metal technology. A scripted effect can create this feature on a habitat when the source body has the orbital deposit. A construction path that calls this effect has not been confirmed in the Stellaris 4.5.4 scripts. [Definition](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/01_planetary_deposits.txt:2552) · [Source body check](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/scripted_effects/federations_event_effects.txt:2424) · [Orbital construction](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/megastructures/habitats.txt:445)

**Habitat Zro Deposits** (`d_hab_zro_1`, `d_hab_zro_2`, `d_hab_zro_3`, `d_hab_zro_4`, `d_hab_zro_5`) are excluded from the PDE manual menu. With Zro Distillation, they produce 1 to 5 zro per month according to deposit size. All five have zero random generation weight. A scripted effect copies the source body's deposit onto the habitat. No call to this chain was found in the Stellaris 4.5.4 construction scripts. [Definitions](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/01_planetary_deposits.txt:2318) · [Deposit copying](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/scripted_effects/federations_event_effects.txt:2316) · [Major orbital](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/megastructures/habitats.txt:445) · [Minor orbital](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/megastructures/habitats.txt:493)

**Habitat Dark Matter Deposits** (`d_hab_dark_matter_1`, `d_hab_dark_matter_2`, `d_hab_dark_matter_3`, `d_hab_dark_matter_10`) are excluded from the PDE manual menu. With Dark Matter Drawing, they produce 1, 2, 3 or 10 dark matter per month according to size. All four have zero random generation weight. A scripted effect copies the source body’s orbital deposit onto the habitat at the same size. No call to this chain was found in the Stellaris 4.5.4 construction scripts. [Definitions](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/01_planetary_deposits.txt:2448) · [Deposit copying](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/scripted_effects/federations_event_effects.txt:2376) · [Major orbital](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/megastructures/habitats.txt:445) · [Minor orbital](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/megastructures/habitats.txt:493)

**Habitat Nanite Deposits** are excluded from the PDE manual menu. Monthly yields are shown in parentheses — `d_hab_nanites_3` (3), `d_hab_harvester_nanites_1` (0.1), `d_hab_harvester_nanites_2` (1.6), `d_hab_harvester_nanites_3` (25.2). All four have zero generation weight. A scripted effect maps the basic deposit and the three harvester sizes on the source body to their habitat equivalents. No call to this chain was found in the Stellaris 4.5.4 construction scripts. The large deposit produces 25.6 nanites on the source body and 25.2 on the habitat. [Habitat features](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/01_planetary_deposits.txt:2754) · [Copying](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/scripted_effects/federations_event_effects.txt:2531) · [Construction](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/megastructures/habitats.txt:445) · [Definition](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/13_machine_age_deposits.txt:333)

**Habitat astral thread deposits** (`d_hab_astral_threads_1`, `d_hab_astral_threads_2`, `d_hab_astral_threads_3`) are excluded from the manual PDE menu. With Astral Harvesting, they produce 1, 2 or 3 threads per month respectively. All three have zero generation weight and grant zero district capacity. A scripted effect copies the source orbital deposit onto the habitat at the same size. No call to this chain was found in the Stellaris 4.5.4 habitat or orbital construction scripts. [Habitat features](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/11_astral_planes_deposits.txt:140) · [Deposit copying](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/scripted_effects/federations_event_effects.txt:2613) · [Habitat construction](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/megastructures/habitats.txt:249) · [Major orbital](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/megastructures/habitats.txt:445) · [Minor orbital](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/megastructures/habitats.txt:493)

**Monuments without active placement.** All three deposits have zero generation weight in Stellaris 4.5.4. The vanilla scripts contain no active placement for these IDs.

- `d_dragon_monument` — Dragonslayer Monument defines 15 unity per month. Defeating the Ether Drake awards the relic `r_dragon_trophy`. [Definition](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/03_leviathans_deposits.txt:28) · [Event](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/leviathans_events_1.txt:9084)
- `d_stellarite_trophy` — Stellar Devourer Trophy defines 5 unity per month and +5 percentage points of happiness. The command placing it on the capital is commented out. The active event options offer a special project or a parade. [Definition](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/03_leviathans_deposits.txt:46) · [Event](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/leviathans_events_2.txt:886)
- `d_sentinels_monument` — The Sentinels defines 15 unity per month. Sparing the Sentinels at the dig site grants the deposit `d_sentinels` and the planet modifier `sentinels_planet_modifier`. [Definition](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/02_event_planetary_deposits.txt:1198) · [Event](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/ancient_relics_arcsite_events_1.txt:5866)

## Agreed classifications

The tables retain the categories of 531 Stellaris 4.5.4 deposits. Of these, 529 have manual placement targets and two describe astral rifts. Categories identify how the feature appears or which game mechanic grants it. District counts give changes to capacity.

Of 593 unique IDs, the planetary decision offers 529 for placement. The 63 removal-only IDs include the two astral rift deposits. Each of the 592 substantive IDs is eligible for removal when present on the selected body. The `d_null_deposit` placeholder is counted separately.

### Volcanic worlds

Seventeen features can generate on a Volcanic World (`pc_volcanic`) and increase the capacity for mining districts, Thermotechnic Forums or both. A separate script places starting features during homeworld setup.

**Buzzing Plains** is included on the strength of its scripted conditions. It has a weight of 0.01 on Gaia Worlds (`pc_gaia`). A developer comment explains that the nonzero weight is needed for terraforming swaps. Natural generation remains unconfirmed in play. [Generation conditions](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/01_planetary_deposits.txt:1631)

**Dust Caverns and Dust Desert** immediately add capacity for three generator districts each. Once the owner researches `tech_mine_volatile_motes`, technicians also produce volatile motes. [Dust Caverns effects](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/01_planetary_deposits.txt:1138) · [Dust Desert effects](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/01_planetary_deposits.txt:1193)

Natural Farmland has a weight of 0.01 on Gaia Worlds and participates in terraforming swaps. When the phase-shifted planet becomes a Gaia World, its event directly adds two instances. [Phase shift](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/distant_stars_events_1.txt:9360)

Both gas swamp deposits have zero random generation weight in systems flagged `empire_home_system`, `sol_system` or `deneb_system`. Their +3 agriculture district capacity applies immediately. [Bubbling Swamp](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/01_planetary_deposits.txt:1276) · [Fuming Bog](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/01_planetary_deposits.txt:1338)

### Orbital deposits

#### Energy

All ten deposits supply a mining station with Energy Credits as their sole resource. The ID suffix gives base monthly output. All ten specify `is_for_colonizable = no`. A separate modifier, `district_hab_energy_max_add`, increases the Reactor District cap for a habitat in the system.

**PDE manual placement.** All amounts are available on the following uncolonizable bodies — `pc_a_star`, `pc_b_star`, `pc_f_star`, `pc_g_star`, `pc_k_star`, `pc_m_star`, `pc_m_giant_star`, `pc_t_star`, `pc_gas_giant`, `pc_molten`, `pc_ice_asteroid`. [can_have_energy_deposits](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/scripted_triggers/00_scripted_triggers.txt:2464)

Ordinary generation can select the +2, +3, +4 and +5 deposits. Eligible bodies are A, B, F, G, K, M and T stars, including M-class red giants, plus gas giants, molten worlds and ice asteroids. The +1 deposit uses the same body filter with zero weight. The +6 through +10 deposits have zero weight and `potential = { always = no }`. Scripts can place these denominations explicitly. [Body classes](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/scripted_triggers/00_scripted_triggers.txt:2464) · [Example placement of +10](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/scripted_effects/01_start_of_game_effects.txt:2549)

Classification follows the extraction method. For example, an anomaly on a colonizable Volcanic World can place the same orbital `d_energy_4` deposit there. [Anomaly condition](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/anomalies/103_anomaly_categories_infernals.txt:75) · [Placement of +4](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/infernals_1_events.txt:2196)

#### Minerals

Each deposit supplies a mining station with the indicated amount of minerals per month. All ten specify `is_for_colonizable = no`. A separate modifier, `district_hab_mining_max_add`, increases Astro-Mining Bay capacity for a habitat in the system.

**PDE manual placement.** All amounts are available on the following uncolonizable bodies — `pc_asteroid`, `pc_molten`, `pc_toxic`, `pc_frozen`, `pc_barren`, `pc_barren_cold`, `pc_broken`, `pc_shattered`, `pc_shattered_2`, `pc_junk`. [can_have_mineral_deposits](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/scripted_triggers/00_scripted_triggers.txt:2449)

Ordinary generation selects +2, +3, +4 and +5. Their base weights on asteroids are multiplied by 2, 3, 4 and 5 respectively. On molten, toxic and frozen worlds the multiplier is 0.5. The +1 deposit uses the same body filter with zero weight. The +6 through +10 deposits have zero weight and `potential = { always = no }`. Scripts explicitly place the denominations with zero weight. [Eligible body classes](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/scripted_triggers/00_scripted_triggers.txt:2449) · [Placement of +1](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/solar_system_initializers/00_nomad_custom_initializers.txt:162)

Scripted placements can also reach colonizable worlds. Archaeology event `ancrel.106` selects an uncolonized body for an anomaly. One outcome places `d_minerals_8` if the body has no deposit for a mining or research station. Shroud event `shroud.3046` can add `d_minerals_10` to a body with a mining deposit or no deposits. AI anomaly event `aianom.6` can add +2, +3 or +4 to a body with an existing mining deposit. [Anomaly target](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/ancient_relics_arcsite_events_1.txt:885) · [Outcome for +8](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/ancient_relics_arcsite_events_1.txt:1022) · [Shroud](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/shroud_events.txt:5609) · [AI anomaly conditions](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/anomalies/99_anomaly_categories_ai.txt:46) · [AI anomaly outcomes](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/anomaly_events_AI.txt:316)

#### Food

All six ordinary orbital food deposits give a mining station the base monthly output shown below. Only +3 can appear through ordinary random generation. The other five have `potential = { always = no }`. None adds habitat district capacity. [Definitions](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/01_orbital_deposits.txt:1789)

**PDE manual placement.** All amounts are available on the following uncolonizable bodies — `pc_ice_asteroid`, `pc_frozen`, `pc_gas_giant`. [can_have_food_deposits](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/scripted_triggers/00_scripted_triggers.txt:2590)

| Deposit ID | Name | Placement in Stellaris 4.5.4 | Vanilla targets for this amount | Where to add |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `d_food_3` | Food +3 | Within nebulae, the generation weight is 0.1 on gas giants, frozen worlds and ice asteroids. Outside nebulae the weight is zero. Special systems, anomalies and a nomad contract can also place it. [Body classes](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/scripted_triggers/00_scripted_triggers.txt:2590) · [Anomaly](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/anomaly_events_4.txt:2741) · [Nomad system](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/solar_system_initializers/00_nomad_custom_initializers.txt:703) | `pc_gas_giant`, `pc_ice_asteroid`, `pc_frozen` [Source](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/01_orbital_deposits.txt:1789) | `can_have_food_deposits = yes` |
| `d_food_4` | Food +4 | Placed during setup of the Starlit Citadel system or certain fallen hive systems. [Citadel](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/solar_system_initializers/biogenesis_initializers.txt:63) · [Fallen hive](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/solar_system_initializers/fallen_empire_initializers.txt:5228) | `pc_asteroid`, `pc_gas_giant` [Source](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/solar_system_initializers/fallen_empire_initializers.txt:5222) | `can_have_food_deposits = yes` |
| `d_food_5` | Food +5 | Placed in the same special systems, by the nomad contract or by the Devouring Swarm outcome of the Translucent Evolution anomaly. [Anomaly](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/extreme_frontiers_events.txt:975) | Regular asteroid, gas giant, `pc_barren`, `pc_barren_cold` or a body with orbital food [Source](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/anomalies/103_anomaly_categories_extreme_frontiers.txt:340) | `can_have_food_deposits = yes` |

[Outcome](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/extreme_frontiers_events.txt:719) | `pc_frozen` [Source](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/anomalies/103_anomaly_categories_extreme_frontiers.txt:220) | `can_have_food_deposits = yes` |
| `d_food_7` | Food +7 | Successful completion of the Remote Food Evaluation nomad contract. | Body with an orbital food deposit [Source](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/missions/missions/02_contracts.txt:2847) | `can_have_food_deposits = yes` |
| `d_food_10` | Food +10 | The Devouring Swarm “Feast” option in the Don’t Count Your Planets anomaly. [Outcome](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/anomaly_events_4.txt:3541) | `uninhabitable_regular_planet = yes` [Source](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/scripted_triggers/00_scripted_triggers.txt:511) | `can_have_food_deposits = yes` |

Remote Food Evaluation adds another deposit to a body that already has orbital food. The chances of adding +3, +5 and +7 are 25%, 60% and 15%, respectively. [Contract amounts](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/missions/missions/02_contracts.txt:2847) · [Reward selection](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/inline_scripts/contracts/OnboardResourceContract.txt:148)

#### Alloys

A mining station produces the listed amount of alloys per month. Only the +1 and +2 deposits occur through ordinary random generation. Their weights increase by a factor of 1.5 inside a nebula. [Eligible body classes](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/scripted_triggers/00_scripted_triggers.txt:2572)

**PDE manual placement.** All amounts are available on the following uncolonizable bodies — `pc_asteroid`, `pc_molten`, `pc_junk`, `pc_broken`, `pc_shattered`, `pc_shattered_2`. #### Trade

A mining station produces the listed amount of trade per month. The +2 to +5 deposits can appear through ordinary generation on gas giants, molten worlds, toxic worlds and asteroids. Generation weights double on asteroids and halve on molten worlds. [Eligible body classes](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/scripted_triggers/00_scripted_triggers.txt:2537)

**PDE manual placement.** All amounts are available on the following uncolonizable bodies — `pc_gas_giant`, `pc_molten`, `pc_toxic`, any asteroid (`is_asteroid = yes`). [can_have_trade_deposits](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/scripted_triggers/00_scripted_triggers.txt:2537)

#### Volatile motes

Each deposit supplies a mining station with volatile motes and adds 1 to habitat reactor district capacity regardless of output. The +1 and +2 deposits can appear through ordinary generation on molten worlds, ordinary asteroids and Viridescent Lightbringer stars. Inside a nebula, both generation weights increase by a factor of 1.5. [Eligible body classes](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/scripted_triggers/00_scripted_triggers.txt:2546)

**PDE manual placement.** All amounts are available on the following uncolonizable bodies — `pc_molten`, `pc_asteroid`, `pc_toxoid_star`. [can_have_volatile_motes_deposits](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/scripted_triggers/00_scripted_triggers.txt:2546)

| Deposit ID | Name | Monthly volatile motes | Base generation weight | Source | Vanilla targets for this amount | Where to add |
| --- | --- | ---: | ---: | --- | --- | --- |
| `d_volatile_motes_1` | Volatile motes +1 | +1 | 1.5 | [Definition](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/02_sr_deposits.txt:246) | `pc_asteroid`, `pc_molten`, `pc_toxoid_star` | `can_have_volatile_motes_deposits = yes` |
| `d_volatile_motes_2` | Volatile motes +2 | +2 | 0.5 | [Definition](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/02_sr_deposits.txt:275) | `pc_asteroid`, `pc_molten`, `pc_toxoid_star` | `can_have_volatile_motes_deposits = yes` |
| `d_volatile_motes_3` | Volatile motes +3 | +3 | 0 | [Definition](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/02_sr_deposits.txt:304) | `pc_asteroid`, `pc_barren`, `pc_molten`, `pc_shattered` [Source](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/solar_system_initializers/special_system_initializers.txt:2917) Also the arkship resource-search classes `can_have_volatile_motes_deposits = yes`. [Arkship resource search](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/scripted_effects/nomads_effects.txt:3799) | `can_have_volatile_motes_deposits = yes` |
| `d_volatile_motes_4` | Volatile motes +4 | +4 | 0 | [Definition](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/02_sr_deposits.txt:325) | No class list in the definition or direct placement sources | `can_have_volatile_motes_deposits = yes` |
| `d_volatile_motes_5` | Volatile motes +5 | +5 | 0 | [Definition](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/02_sr_deposits.txt:346) | `pc_shattered` [Source](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/solar_system_initializers/unplugged_initializers.txt:690) Also the arkship resource-search classes `can_have_volatile_motes_deposits = yes`. [Arkship resource search](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/scripted_effects/nomads_effects.txt:3799) | `can_have_volatile_motes_deposits = yes` |

Events and system initializers can place +3. The star in the Toxic God system is one example of explicit +5 placement. No direct placement was found for +4 in the vanilla scripts. The deficit event checks for an existing +4 deposit. [Event placing +3](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/story_events.txt:3192) · [System placing +3](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/solar_system_initializers/hostile_system_initializers.txt:3422) · [Placement of +5](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/solar_system_initializers/toxoid_initializers.txt:169) · [Check for +4](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/situation_deficit_events.txt:1100)

#### Exotic gases

All five deposits supply mining stations with exotic gases. Each adds 1 to habitat reactor district capacity. The +1 and +2 deposits can appear through ordinary generation on gas giants, toxic worlds, ice asteroids and Viridescent Lightbringer stars. Inside a nebula, their generation weights increase by a factor of 1.5. [Eligible body classes](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/scripted_triggers/00_scripted_triggers.txt:2563)

**PDE manual placement.** All amounts are available on the following uncolonizable bodies — `pc_gas_giant`, `pc_toxic`, `pc_ice_asteroid`, `pc_toxoid_star`. [can_have_exotic_gases_deposits](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/scripted_triggers/00_scripted_triggers.txt:2563)

All three deposits with zero generation weight have scripted placements. Event `story.335` can place +3. A gas giant in one of the Chosen systems receives +4, and the star in the Toxic God system receives +5. [Event placing +3](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/story_events.txt:3146) · [Gas giant with +4](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/solar_system_initializers/special_system_initializers.txt:3023) · [Star with +5](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/solar_system_initializers/toxoid_initializers.txt:168)

#### Rare crystals

A mining station produces the listed amount of rare crystals per month. The +1 and +2 deposits can appear through ordinary generation on frozen worlds, ice asteroids and both crystalline asteroid classes. Inside a nebula, their generation weights increase by a factor of 1.5. [Eligible body classes](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/scripted_triggers/00_scripted_triggers.txt:2554)

**PDE manual placement.** All amounts are available on the following uncolonizable bodies — `pc_frozen`, `pc_ice_asteroid`, `pc_rare_crystal_asteroid`, `pc_crystal_asteroid`. [can_have_rare_crystals_deposits](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/scripted_triggers/00_scripted_triggers.txt:2554)

#### Living metal

| --- | --- | ---: | ---: | ---: | --- | --- |
| `d_living_metal_deposit` | Living Metal +1 | +1 | +1 | 0 | [Definition](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/02_sr_deposits.txt:588) · [Mining prerequisite](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/strategic_resources/00_strategic_resources.txt:343) | Asteroids (`is_asteroid = yes`), `pc_barren`, `pc_barren_cold`, `pc_frozen`, `pc_molten`, `pc_toxic`. An ordinary orbital deposit for a mining station. Surveying a barren world or an asteroid can reveal the regular Peculiar Crater anomaly. Completing it triggers A New Metal, which places the deposit. [Anomaly conditions](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/anomalies/00_anomaly_categories.txt:772) · [Survey outcome](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/anomaly_events_2.txt:1000)

The same ID is used by the Here Be Dragons origin, the Starlit Citadel system (three deposits on its central black hole), the Living Metal Lakes event and Prospectorium resource discoveries. #### Zro

Research stations collect zro once the empire researches Zro Distillation. The +1 and +2 deposits can appear through ordinary generation on toxic and shrouded worlds. Outside nebulae, their generation weights are halved. [Extraction prerequisite](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/strategic_resources/00_strategic_resources.txt:367) · [Eligible body classes](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/scripted_triggers/00_scripted_triggers.txt:2583)

**PDE manual placement.** All amounts are available on the following uncolonizable bodies — `pc_toxic`, `pc_shrouded`. [can_have_zro_deposits](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/scripted_triggers/00_scripted_triggers.txt:2583)

Surveying a gas giant without strategic resources can reveal the Ice Ice Giant anomaly if the empire has Zro Distillation or has begun the Zroni chain. Completing the anomaly places a +1, +2 or +3 deposit with respective weights of 16, 4 and 1. [Anomaly conditions](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/anomalies/01_anomaly_categories.txt:360) · [Anomaly outcome](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/anomaly_events_1.txt:2344) · [Deposit size selection](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/scripted_effects/00_scripted_effects.txt:3233)

The Zroni chain places +3 at intermediate dig sites, +4 at the Zroni Core World dig site and +5 on Zron Prime. The +5 deposit also occurs in Shroudwalker enclave systems. When a Shroud Storm turns an eligible body into a shrouded world, it places one of the five deposit sizes with equal probability. [Dig site +3](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/ancient_relics_arcsite_events_1.txt:143) · [Dig site +4](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/ancient_relics_arcsite_events_1.txt:324) · [Zron Prime](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/solar_system_initializers/ancient_relics_initializers.txt:417) · [Enclave](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/solar_system_initializers/overlord_initializers.txt:580) · [Storm outcome](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/cosmic_storms_events_1.txt:4463)

#### Dark matter

Research stations collect dark matter after the empire researches Dark Matter Drawing. The +1 and +2 deposits can occur through ordinary generation only on black holes. A nebula affects the generation weight of the +1 deposit. [Extraction prerequisite](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/strategic_resources/00_strategic_resources.txt:391) · [Body class](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/scripted_triggers/00_scripted_triggers.txt:2598)

Surveying an asteroid without strategic resources can reveal the Radiating Asteroid anomaly. Completing it places a +1, +2 or +3 deposit with respective weights of 16, 4 and 1. The chance of receiving +3 from this outcome is 1 in 21. [Anomaly conditions](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/anomalies/01_anomaly_categories.txt:384) · [Anomaly outcome](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/anomaly_events_1.txt:2378) · [Deposit size selection](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/scripted_effects/00_scripted_effects.txt:3225)

Empires with the Dark Consortium or Shadow Corporation civic also place a +3 deposit on an eligible body at game start. When VLUUR is destroyed, each selected body can receive a +1, +2 or +3 deposit with equal probability. [Empire setup](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/astral_planes_events.txt:1831) · [VLUUR’s destruction](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/galactic_features_events.txt:4063)

Weight 2, rising to 10 on a Nanite World (`pc_gray_goo`). [Definition](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/04_distant_stars_deposits.txt:23) | Uncolonizable non-star body. Both localizations show +1. The deposit definition grants a research station 3 nanites per month per instance. Two instances on one broken world in the Scavenger Bot system yield 6 nanites per month. A Shroud Storm or Cetana’s events can also place this deposit. [Russian name](../../Localisation_Base/1-Prio_Russian_Localization_Fix_RE/russian/megacorp_l_russian.yml:2931) · [English name](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/localisation/english/megacorp_l_english.yml:2931) · [Scavenger Bot](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/solar_system_initializers/distant_stars_initializers.txt:2468) · [Storm](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/cosmic_storms_events_1.txt:4510) · [Cetana](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/machine_age_crisis_events.txt:6693)

#### Physics research

A research station produces the listed amount of physics research each month. Deposits of +2 through +5 can generate randomly. The +2 deposit can appear on A, B, F, G, K, M and T stars, including M-class red giants. The +3 through +5 deposits can also appear on protostars, neutron stars, pulsars, black holes and bodies with the `can_have_physics_deposits` flag. Weights in the table are given before modifiers for the type of celestial body. [Generation conditions](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/scripted_triggers/00_scripted_triggers.txt:2488)

**PDE manual placement.** All amounts are available on the following uncolonizable bodies — `pc_a_star`, `pc_b_star`, `pc_f_star`, `pc_g_star`, `pc_k_star`, `pc_m_star`, `pc_m_giant_star`, `pc_t_star`, `pc_protostar`, `pc_neutron_star`, `pc_pulsar`, `pc_black_hole`, a body with the `can_have_physics_deposits` flag. [can_have_physics_deposits](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/scripted_triggers/00_scripted_triggers.txt:2501)

| Deposit ID | Monthly physics research | Habitat research district capacity | Base weight | Source | Vanilla targets for this amount | Where to add |
| --- | ---: | ---: | ---: | --- | --- | --- |
| `d_physics_1` | +1 | +1 | 0 | [Definition](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/01_orbital_deposits.txt:849) | `pc_b_star`, `pc_a_star`, `pc_f_star`, `pc_g_star`, `pc_k_star`, `pc_m_star`, `pc_m_giant_star`, `pc_t_star` | `can_have_physics_deposits = yes` |

Scripts place deposits of +1 and +6 through +10. Examples include +1 in the geocentric Sol system, +6 for a Machine Intelligence investigating the Singing Planet, and +10 in the Vultaum home system. [Sol system](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/solar_system_initializers/sol_initializers.txt:3844) · [Singing Planet](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/anomaly_events_4.txt:3961) · [Vultaum system](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/solar_system_initializers/prescripted_species_systems.txt:1320)

`d_physics_25` belongs to the same resource series. Its only vanilla source is the Life in a Cage outcome of the Entangled Dark Matter rift. The event places `d_physics_25` and `d_engineering_25` together and starts a timer for 3,600–3,960 days. The follow-up event removes both deposits. Placing the deposit manually does not start this timer. [Placement and timer](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/astral_rifts_2_events.txt:2119) · [Removal](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/astral_rifts_2_events.txt:2150)

#### Society research

A research station produces the listed amount of society research each month. The +2 through +5 deposits can generate on gas giants, toxic worlds and shrouded worlds. Event scripts or system initializers place the +1 and +6 through +10 deposits. [Generation conditions](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/scripted_triggers/00_scripted_triggers.txt:2480) · [Placement sources](../../TMP/PDE/v4.5.4/CATALOG_EN.md)

**PDE manual placement.** All amounts are available on the following uncolonizable bodies — `pc_toxic`, `pc_shrouded`, `pc_gas_giant`. [can_have_society_deposits](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/scripted_triggers/00_scripted_triggers.txt:2480)

The timers for +15 and +25 are started by their respective rift events. Manual placement through PDE does not start either timer. [First outcome](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/astral_rifts_3_events.txt:799) · [Second outcome](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/astral_rifts_3_events.txt:826) · [Removal](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/astral_rifts_3_events.txt:851)

#### Engineering research

A research station produces the listed amount of engineering research per month. The +2 deposit can generate on frozen worlds and gas giants. The +3 through +5 deposits can also generate on protostars, neutron stars, pulsars and black holes. On neutron stars, pulsars and black holes, the base weight for each of these three deposits is multiplied by 5. Events or system initializers place +1 and +6 through +10. [Eligible bodies](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/scripted_triggers/00_scripted_triggers.txt:2519) · [Placement sources](../../TMP/PDE/v4.5.4/CATALOG_EN.md)

**PDE manual placement.** All amounts are available on the following uncolonizable bodies — `pc_frozen`, `pc_gas_giant`, `pc_protostar`, `pc_neutron_star`, `pc_pulsar`, `pc_black_hole`. The description of `d_engineering_8` mentions a plain covered in wreckage (`d_engineering_8_desc`). Two events place this deposit. In Drifting Battlefield, choosing to study the records after decoding the beacon signal places it. At the end of The Library dig site, choosing quarantine replaces the existing deposits with three separate +8 deposits, one for each research field. [Description](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/localisation/english/megacorp_l_english.yml:2825) · [Drifting Battlefield](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/anomaly_events_2.txt:3034) · [The Library](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/ancient_relics_arcsite_events_2.txt:8530)

The Life in a Cage outcome of the Entangled Dark Matter rift places `d_engineering_25` and `d_physics_25` together. The rift event starts a timer for 3,600–3,960 days, after which a follow-up event removes both deposits. [Placement and timer](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/astral_rifts_2_events.txt:2119) · [Removal](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/astral_rifts_2_events.txt:2150)

#### Minor artifacts

All three series share the in-game name “Minor Artifacts Deposit”. The number in the ID gives the base monthly output. Random generation is disabled. With Ancient Relics, completing a dig site or an eligible precursor special project selects a series based on the body. On an uncolonizable body that is not a terraforming candidate, the research series takes priority if a research station deposit is present or the body has no deposits. An existing mining station deposit selects the mining series. Other bodies use the planetary series. The check uses the existing deposit type regardless of whether a station has been built. [Dig site completion](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/ancient_relics_site_spawn_event.txt:79) · [Precursor projects](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/ancient_relics_site_spawn_event.txt:150)

For dig sites, the base weights for no deposit, +1, +2 and +3 are 33, 54, 11 and 3. For eligible precursor projects, the respective probabilities are 75%, 20%, 4% and 1%. [Dig site weights](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/inline_scripts/deposits/minor_artifacts_randomiser.txt:5) · [Project weights](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/ancient_relics_site_spawn_event.txt:170)

At the end of the Anomalous Cube dig site, the game places living metal and can add a mining-series deposit if the body has no minor artifacts deposit. A successful scan by an arkship with Akashic Whisperings can also uncover either the mining or the research series. [Anomalous Cube](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/strange_worlds_events.txt:3401) · [Arkship effect](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/scripted_effects/nomads_effects.txt:3530)

#### Astral threads

Research stations collect astral threads after the owner researches Astral Harvesting. All three deposits have zero random generation weight. They are placed when a scar or rift forms, or as an anomaly outcome. [Resource requirements](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/strategic_resources/00_strategic_resources.txt:466)

| Deposit ID | Monthly astral threads | Habitat research district capacity | Placement | Where to add |
| --- | ---: | ---: | --- | --- |
| `d_astral_threads_deposit_1` | +1 | +1 | Each newly created Astral Scar receives +1, including scars placed during galaxy generation. The Ancient Rift at the center of the Formless system azilash also starts with +1. [Definition](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/11_astral_planes_deposits.txt:3) · [Scar creation](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/scripted_effects/astral_planes_effects.txt:22) · [Galaxy generation](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/astral_planes_events.txt:25) · [azilash](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/solar_system_initializers/astral_planes_initializers.txt:17) | `pc_astral_scar`, `pc_rift_star` |
| `d_astral_threads_deposit_2` | +2 | +2 | The +2 deposit appears when an astral rift spawns, before exploration begins. The placement event skips a rift flagged `crystal_rift`. [Definition](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/11_astral_planes_deposits.txt:22) · [Rift spawn](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/on_actions/00_on_actions.txt:4973) · [Placement](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/astral_planes_events.txt:711) | Hidden from planetary-decision placement. Vanilla places this deposit on an `astral_rift` object. [Vanilla placement](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/astral_planes_events.txt:722) |
| `d_astral_threads_deposit_3` | +3 | +2 | In the Black Hole Instability anomaly, choosing to collect the thread from a safe distance places +3 on the black hole. After 3–10 years, if the science ship’s empire owns the system, the follow-up event removes +3 and creates a scar in the system with its own +1 deposit. The anomaly choice starts the timer. [Definition](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/11_astral_planes_deposits.txt:41) · [Anomaly conditions](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/anomalies/101_anomaly_categories_astral_planes.txt:62) · [Choice and timer](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/astral_planes_events.txt:4359) · [Removal and scar](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/astral_planes_events.txt:4410) | `pc_black_hole` |

#### Unique orbital deposits

The unique +10 deposit in the Great Wound system is listed under Orbital deposits → Dark matter. [Deposit entry](#dark-matter)

| `d_consumer_goods_obsessional_directive` | Spire of Commodities | Obsessional Directive | 2 consumer goods | — | On game start, the Obsessional Directive civic places this deposit on an eligible body in the capital system. After the empire meets its Commodities Consolidation quota, the storage protocols option can place another deposit. Settled empires select a body within their borders, nomadic empires select one in a waystation system. Output is independent of the current owner’s civics. The related habitat feature `d_hab_consumer_goods_2` is listed under menu exclusions. ##### Nanotech

A starbase Nanite Harvester or a waystation Nanite Hub creates small deposits on eligible bodies. All three sizes have zero ordinary generation weight. Output is per deposit per month at a mining station. [Harvester](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/starbase_buildings/00_starbase_buildings.txt:1626) · [Hub](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/starbase_modules/00_waystation_modules.txt:3843) · [Placement](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/machine_age_events_2.txt:1189)

| Deposit ID | Name | Nanites per month | Source | Where to add |
| --- | --- | ---: | --- | --- |
| `d_nanite_harvester_deposit` | Nanotech Deposit 1 | 0.1 | [Definition](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/13_machine_age_deposits.txt:303) | `can_have_mineral_deposits = yes` [Source](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/machine_age_events_2.txt:1196) |
| `d_nanite_harvester_deposit_regular` | Nanotech Deposit 2 | 1.6 | [Definition](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/13_machine_age_deposits.txt:318) | `can_have_mineral_deposits = yes` [Source](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/machine_age_events_2.txt:1196) |
| `d_nanite_harvester_deposit_large` | Nanotech Deposit 3 | 25.6 | [Definition](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/13_machine_age_deposits.txt:333) | `can_have_mineral_deposits = yes` [Source](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/machine_age_events_2.txt:1196) |

Once the Nanotech tradition tree is complete, the game checks deposit growth every five years. It adds instances and replaces smaller deposits with larger ones according to body size and growth stage. Removing the harvester or hub clears all three deposit types from the system. [Interval](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/on_actions/00_on_actions.txt:285) · [Growth conditions](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/machine_age_events_2.txt:1355) · [Removal](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/machine_age_events_2.txt:1210)

An arkship can also place these deposits independently. At the end of Nanite Consumption it turns the planet into a Nanite World, clears the original deposits, places one small deposit and rolls for additional deposits. The outcomes include medium and large deposits. [Consumption outcome](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/nomads_events_1.txt:15357)

The wind creatures’ contribution to the district cap depends on the planet’s modifiers. The base value is +5, rising to +7.5 with Mastery of Nature, +8.75 with Cultivated Worldscaping, or +11.25 if both modifiers are present. These are the scripted values before in-game display. [Formula](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/script_values/00_script_values.txt:2732)

On `pc_volcanic`, the volcanic hive contributes +5 mining districts without modifiers, +7.5 with Mastery of Nature, +8.75 with Cultivated Worldscaping, and +11.25 with both modifiers. These are the scripted values before in-game display. If the volcanic ID is present on another planet class, it contributes +5. The normal decision on such a planet places the ordinary hive. [Formula](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/script_values/00_script_values.txt:2732)

#### Colony events → The Doorway

| Deposit ID | Name | Placement and jobs | Where to add |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `d_portal_research_zone` | Portal Research Area | The event places the complex and starts a separate portal research project. A single PDE menu entry grants 200 physicist jobs or their equivalents. Manual placement on a world without portal flags uses the base mode. [Definition](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/02_event_planetary_deposits.txt:1008) · [Placement and project](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/colony_events_3.txt:1214) | Colonizable world except `pc_habitat`, `pc_ringworld_habitable`, `pc_shattered_ring_habitable` |

| Mode | Planet flags | Output added to physicists by the deposit |
| --- | --- | --- |
| No special effect | — | +4 physics |
| Closed | `dportal_closed` | +1 physics (+4 base and −3 for closure) |
| Energy portal | `dportal_energy` | +4 physics and +3 energy |
| Null Void | `dportal_void` | +4 physics and +0.1 dark matter |
| Enhanced Null Void | `dportal_void` and `dportal_void2` | +4 physics and +0.6 dark matter (+0.1 and +0.5) |
| Hell portal | `dportal_hell` | +4 physics while open, higher upkeep |

The base upkeep increase for physicists is 1.5 consumer goods for a non-gestalt, 4 minerals for a hive or 3 energy for a machine intelligence. With the hell flag this rises to 2.5 consumer goods, 6 minerals or 4.5 energy respectively. [Upkeep modifiers](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/02_event_planetary_deposits.txt:1046)

The flags are checked independently. Enhancing the Null Void adds the second flag while retaining the first. Accepting refugees can set the closed flag while retaining hell upkeep. [Null Void](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/colony_events_3.txt:1651) · [Enhancement](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/colony_events_3.txt:1711) · [Accepting refugees](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/colony_events_3.txt:2797)

#### Machine ascension → Transformation

On the first stage of Transformation, the result of nanotech research determines which replicator appears on the capital. Choosing Nanotech sets the empire flag `transformation_nanotech` and triples output. A replicator without an owner produces nothing. [Nanotech choice](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/machine_age_situation_events_3.txt:705)

| Deposit ID | Name | Nanotech discoveries | Nanites per month | With Nanotech | Sources | Where to add |
| --- | --- | --- | ---: | ---: | --- | --- |
| `d_nanites_medium` | Nanite Replicator Omega | Groundbreaking | 1.2 | 3.6 | [Definition](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/13_machine_age_deposits.txt:203) · [Placement](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/machine_age_situation_events_3.txt:146) | Colonizable world |
| `d_nanites_small` | Nanite Replicator Alpha | Promising | 0.6 | 1.8 | [Definition](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/13_machine_age_deposits.txt:236) · [Placement](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/machine_age_situation_events_3.txt:184) | Colonizable world |
| `d_nanites_tiny` | Nanite Replicator Beta | Noteworthy | 0.3 | 0.9 | [Definition](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/13_machine_age_deposits.txt:269) · [Placement](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/machine_age_situation_events_3.txt:223) | Colonizable world |

In the second stage of Transformation, the research outcome determines which size of vat is placed on the capital. Each ID has two monthly output values. Choosing modularity sets the `transformation_modularity` flag for the empire and triples the output of its vats. Vats without an owner produce nothing. [Second stage](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/machine_age_situation_events_3.txt:233) · [Modularity choice](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/machine_age_situation_events_3.txt:713)

| Deposit ID | Name | Research outcome | Monthly living metal | With modularity | Sources | Where to add |
| --- | --- | --- | ---: | ---: | --- | --- |
| `d_living_metal_medium` | Living Metal Vats | Groundbreaking | 1.2 | 3.6 | [Definition](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/13_machine_age_deposits.txt:350) · [Placement](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/machine_age_situation_events_3.txt:367) | Colonizable world |
| `d_living_metal_small` | Small Living Metal Vats | Acceptable | 0.6 | 1.8 | [Definition](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/13_machine_age_deposits.txt:383) · [Placement](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/machine_age_situation_events_3.txt:405) | Colonizable world |
| `d_living_metal_tiny` | Miniature Living Metal Vats | Modest | 0.3 | 0.9 | [Definition](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/13_machine_age_deposits.txt:416) · [Placement](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/machine_age_situation_events_3.txt:444) | Colonizable world |

The third stage of the Transformation situation places a virtual power plant on the capital according to the virtuality research outcome. The table gives direct monthly output. Choosing virtuality sets `transformation_virtuality`. Output increases while the current owner has this flag and is zero without an owner. [Stage events](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/situations/08_machine_age_situations.txt:1881) · [Virtuality choice](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/machine_age_situation_events_3.txt:718)

| `d_virtual_power_small` | Compact Virtual Power Plant | Promising | 12 / 0.2 | 36 / 0.6 | [Definition](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/13_machine_age_deposits.txt:486) · [Placement](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/machine_age_situation_events_3.txt:626) | Colonizable world |

### Planetary deposits

#### Ordinary

| Deposit ID | Name | Subcategory | Placement and effects | Where to add |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `d_alien_pets_deposit` | Isolated Valley | Rare fauna | Can generate on non-volcanic worlds eligible for habitable deposits. Gaia worlds double the weight. The empire home system, Sol and Deneb flags set it to zero. Anomalies can also place it. The valley enables the Alien Zoo for regular empires. Genesis Guides and equivalent civics gain +10% unity from jobs and +1,000 amenities. [Definition](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/01_planetary_deposits.txt:1563) · [Zoo conditions](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/buildings/10_deposit_buildings.txt:206) · [Anomaly](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/anomaly_events_1.txt:2577) | `pc_desert`, `pc_arid`, `pc_savannah`, `pc_tropical`, `pc_continental`, `pc_ocean`, `pc_tundra`, `pc_arctic`, `pc_alpine`, `pc_nuked`, `pc_gaia`, `pc_relic` |

#### Civics

| Deposit ID | Name | Civic | Conditions and effects | Where to add |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `d_genesis_preserve` | Genesis Preserve | Genesis Guides and its variants | The preserve appears on the starting capital, through Genesis Ark colonization or by planetary decision. Total district capacity −2. Each point on the empire counter adds 2.5% unity and society research from jobs, or 5% on a Gaia world, up to 10 points. Uplifting the species replaces the preserve with a monument. A cleanup event removes the preserve if the world lacks an owner with a qualifying civic. [Definition](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/13_machine_age_deposits.txt:120) · [Capital](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/game_start.txt:591) · [Colonization](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/machine_age_events.txt:173) · [Decision](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/decisions/12_machine_age_decisions.txt:5) · [Counter](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/script_values/04_script_values_machine_age.txt:343) · [Uplift](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/machine_age_events.txt:273) · [Preserve removal](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/machine_age_events.txt:201) | Colonizable world [Source](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/decisions/12_machine_age_decisions.txt:21) |
| `d_genesis_monument` | Genesis Monument | Genesis Guides and its variants | Uplifting a pre-sapient species replaces the Genesis Preserve with the monument. Genesis Guides and equivalent civics gain +25% unity from jobs and 200 biologist jobs or their drone equivalents. Other owners gain +10% unity from jobs. These bonuses are fixed. [Definition](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/13_machine_age_deposits.txt:156) · [Preserve replacement](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/machine_age_events.txt:272) | Colonizable world [Source](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/machine_age_events.txt:245) |

#### Minor artifacts

On a colonizable world, a terraforming candidate, or another body outside the two orbital branches, dig sites and precursor projects can place a rare planetary feature. On a colony the feature produces minor artifacts directly. All three sizes give zero additional district capacity. [Placement after excavation](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/ancient_relics_site_spawn_event.txt:129) · [Placement after a project](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/ancient_relics_site_spawn_event.txt:200)

| Deposit ID | Monthly minor artifacts on the colony | Source | Where to add |
| --- | ---: | --- | --- |
| `d_artifacts_planet_1` | +1 | [Definition](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/06_ancient_relics_deposits.txt:428) | Colonizable planet or a remaining-branch body: a terraforming candidate or a body with another deposit type [Source](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/ancient_relics_site_spawn_event.txt:88) |
| `d_artifacts_planet_2` | +2 | [Definition](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/06_ancient_relics_deposits.txt:443) | Colonizable planet or a remaining-branch body: a terraforming candidate or a body with another deposit type [Source](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/ancient_relics_site_spawn_event.txt:88) |
| `d_artifacts_planet_3` | +3 | [Definition](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/06_ancient_relics_deposits.txt:458) | Colonizable planet or a remaining-branch body: a terraforming candidate or a body with another deposit type [Source](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/ancient_relics_site_spawn_event.txt:88) |

A separate scripted effect maps a source body’s `d_artifacts_research_*` deposit to a `d_artifacts_planet_*` feature of the same size on the habitat. A construction path to this effect has not been confirmed in the vanilla Stellaris 4.5.4 scripts. [Deposit copying](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/scripted_effects/federations_event_effects.txt:2577) · [Construction](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/megastructures/habitats.txt:445)

[Initializer](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/solar_system_initializers/grand_archive_initializers.txt:148) |
| `d_ruined_hive_dwelling` | Ruined Hive Mind Dwelling | Treasure Hunters | — | The return of the Black Needle replaces the original dwelling with its ruins and unlocks colonization. Grants 10 monthly unity and +5% society research from jobs. [Definition](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/14_grand_archive_deposits.txt:130) · [Dwelling replacement](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/origin_events_grand_archive.txt:4205) | `pc_savannah` |
| `d_landed_dragon` | Feeding Dragon | Here Be Dragons | — | The Sky Dragon lands on the capital. While it feeds, the planet gains 5 monthly unity and +5 stability at an upkeep of 15 food per month. The departure event removes the deposit. [Definition](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/02_event_planetary_deposits.txt:1386) · [Landing](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/scripted_effects/00_scripted_effects.txt:5661) · [Departure](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/aquatics_events.txt:915) | Colonizable world |
| `d_shroud_rift_deposit` | Shroud-Touched Region | Shroud-Forged | — | Four regions appear on the starting capital. Each produces 2 zro per month (8 in total at game start). For Shroud-Forged owners, each also adds +5% Deviancy until the liberation path is complete. The Pierce the Shroud decision can create another region. When Shroud creatures occupy a planet, they gradually replace other features with these regions. [Definition](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/02_event_planetary_deposits.txt:1595) · [Starting deposits](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/shroud_forged_events.txt:31) · [Starting count](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/scripted_variables/09_scripted_variables_shroud.txt:227) · [Decision](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/decisions/02_special_decisions.txt:2660) · [Occupation](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/shroud_forged_events.txt:1726) | Natural colonizable world |
| `d_shroud_flora_deposit` | Semi-Shrouded Region | Shroud-Forged | — | Choosing to preserve and study the region in Shrouded Organisms adds it to a colony with Shroud-Forged pops. An existing rift is optional. The region grants 1 zro per month, −2 total district capacity and +10% society research from jobs. [Definition](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/02_event_planetary_deposits.txt:1626) · [Event](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/shroud_forged_events.txt:3358) | Colonizable world |

In a Shroud-Forged campaign, Shroud Malady can target a colony with Shroud-Forged pops if it contains either a Shroud-Touched Region or a Semi-Shrouded Region. Manually placed features also satisfy this condition. [Event conditions](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/shroud_forged_events.txt:3717)

### Blockers

The Stellaris 4.5.4 catalog marks 134 deposits as blockers. The register includes 127 of them and lists the other 7 under manual menu exclusions. [Parser catalog](../../TMP/PDE/v4.5.4/deposits.json) · [PDE menu exclusions](#pde-menu-exclusions)

Arctic and alpine worlds have twice the weight of tundra worlds. Total district cap −1. Clearing requires Climate Control Network, 500 energy credits and 180 days. [Definition](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/01_blocker_deposits.txt:624) | `pc_tundra`, `pc_arctic`, `pc_alpine` |
