| Shroudstone conditions | Mining districts | Habitat astro-mining bays | Total district cap |
| --- | ---: | ---: | ---: |
| No owner | 0 | 0 | 0 |
| Owner has neither the Composer of Strands covenant nor the `forging_our_own_path` flag | 0 | 0 | −1 |
| Covenant or flag, eligible natural or relic world | +1 | 0 | 0 |
| Covenant or flag, habitat | 0 | +1 | 0 |
| Covenant or flag, other world class | 0 | 0 | 0 |

| Shroud flora conditions (per blocker) | Agriculture districts | Total district cap | Direct zro output | Zro from farmers |
| --- | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: |
| No owner | 0 | 0 | +1 | 0 |
| Owner has neither the Composer of Strands covenant nor the `forging_our_own_path` flag | 0 | −1 | +1 | 0 |
| Covenant or flag, world matches `habitable_planet_not_urban` or is `pc_relic` | +1 | 0 | +1 | +0.05 |
| Covenant or flag, other world class | 0 | 0 | +1 | +0.05 |

#### Volcanic

| Deposit ID | Name | Total district cap | Conditions and effects | Where to add |
| --- | --- | ---: | --- | --- |
| `d_raging_lavafalls` | Raging Lavafalls | −2 | Generates only on volcanic worlds with at least two free city districts, at base weight 7. Biologists produce extra energy when the ranger bonus applies. Clearing requires Deep Crust Engineering, 1,000 energy credits and 270 days. [Definition](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/15_infernals_deposits.txt:823) | `pc_volcanic` |
| `d_rugged_landscape` | Rugged Landscape | −2 | Generates only on volcanic worlds with at least two free city districts, at base weight 7. Biologists produce extra minerals when the ranger bonus applies. Clearing requires Deep Crust Engineering, 1,000 energy credits and 270 days. [Definition](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/15_infernals_deposits.txt:874) | `pc_volcanic` |

#### Utility

| Deposit ID | Name | Total district cap | Conditions and effects | Where to add |
| --- | --- | ---: | --- | --- |
| `d_mothballed_facilities` | Mothballed Facilities | −2 | In the Horizon Signal event The Waiting World, the 100-mineral option clears the blocker, reveals `d_worm_mine` and `d_worm_farm`, and builds three mining and three agriculture districts. The other option removes the blocker without this reward. Normal clearing is disabled. Placing it in PDE does not start the event. [Definition](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/02_event_planetary_deposits.txt:667) · [Event](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/horizonsignal_events.txt:2656) · [Alternative outcome](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/horizonsignal_events.txt:2680) | Colonizable world except `pc_habitat`, `pc_ringworld_habitable`, `pc_shattered_ring_habitable`, `pc_city`, `pc_relic` [Source](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/horizonsignal_events.txt:2612) |
| `d_spawning_complex_blocker` | Overlord Spawning Complex | −1 | Building the Splinter Hive holding places the complex on the subject planet. Demolishing the holding removes it. Normal clearing is disabled. Manual placement occupies one district slot. The holding provides the jobs and the link to the overlord’s capital. [Definition](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/08_overlord_deposits.txt:222) · [Construction and demolition](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/buildings/15_overlord_holdings.txt:600) | Colonizable world |
| `d_wilderness_glade_blocker` | Invasive Thicket | −1 | The Wilderness Glade holding places this blocker on the subject planet when built and removes it when demolished. Normal clearing is disabled. Manual placement only applies the district penalty. The espionage blocker with the same name is recorded under `d_wilderness_root_blocker`. [Definition](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/12_wilderness_deposits.txt:302) · [Source](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/buildings/15_overlord_holdings.txt:2337) | Colonizable world |
| `d_nature_preserve_blocker` | Nature Preserve | −1 | The overlord's Ranger Lodge holding places this blocker on the subject's planet when built and removes it when demolished. Normal clearing is disabled. The civic uses `d_big_nature_preserve_blocker` on the empire's own planets. [Definition](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/08_overlord_deposits.txt:245) · [Placement](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/buildings/15_overlord_holdings.txt:1557) · [Holding demolition](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/buildings/15_overlord_holdings.txt:1563) | Colonizable world |
| `d_resource_consolidation_1` | Malfunctioning Replicator Bay | −1 | Clearing costs 600 minerals and takes 360 days. No placement was found in the 4.5.4 scripts. The AI budget checks for this ID. The Resource Consolidation start places Prototype Machinery and two instances of Unfinished Construction. [Definition](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/07_federations_deposits.txt:1057) · [AI budget](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/ai_budget/00_minerals_budget.txt:405) · [Placement](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/inline_scripts/game_start/origin_machine.txt:14) | `pc_machine` (Machine World). PDE designates this class for manual placement of the utility blocker. |

#### Blocker clearing results

This table lists planetary features revealed by clearing a blocker. District bonuses apply to the revealed feature.

| Deposit ID | Name | Generator districts | Mining districts | Agriculture districts | How it is revealed and what it grants | Where to add |
| --- | --- | ---: | ---: | ---: | --- | --- |
| `d_organic_landfill` | Organic Landfills | +4 | — | — | Clearing `d_former_battlefield` always reveals Organic Landfills. Clearing `d_machine_empire_ruins` or `d_assimilators_ruins` can reveal either Organic Landfills or `d_metal_boneyard`. The collided planet and the Unplugged start can both have `d_former_battlefield`. The Remnants origin event `origin.3101` can also place the deposit directly. Society research from jobs +10%. [Battlefield clearing](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/01_blocker_deposits.txt:1152) · [Event](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/origin_events_3.txt:262) · [Starting blockers](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/scripted_effects/01_start_of_game_effects.txt:851) · [Effect](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/02_event_planetary_deposits.txt:722) · [Collided planet](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/solar_system_initializers/special_system_initializers.txt:3934) · [Unplugged](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/scripted_effects/01_start_of_game_effects.txt:875) | Colonizable world |
| `d_star_mall_promenade` | Promenade | — | — | — | Clearing `d_star_mall_promenade_blocker` on the Star Mall habitat reveals the promenade. Regular empires gain +25% trade from jobs and hives gain +25% amenities. Both gain +10 percentage points of biological pop happiness. Machine intelligences gain +2 total district capacity. Without a qualifying owner there is no bonus. [Definition](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/02_event_planetary_deposits.txt:2142) · [Blocker clearing](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/02_event_planetary_deposits.txt:2126) · [Habitat](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/solar_system_initializers/special_system_initializers.txt:3415) | `pc_habitat` |
| `d_forgotten_level` | Reclaimed Level | — | — | — | Nomads → Forgotten Level. On a non-gestalt arkship, an investigation costing 500 energy credits reveals `d_forgotten_level_blocker`. Clearing it for 1,000 energy credits over 360 days reveals the level, granting +10% housing and +1 artificial world district. Origin-specific bonuses lasting 1,080 days trigger when the blocker is cleared. [Definition](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/17_nomads_deposits.txt:176) · [Investigation](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/events/nomads_arkship_events.txt:6521) · [Clearing and bonuses](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/17_nomads_deposits.txt:189) | Arkship (colony ship) |
| `d_deep_sleep_cryo_pods_renewed` | Renewed Cryo Pods | — | — | — | Nomads → Deep Sleep. Clearing the old cryo pods grants 100 deep-sleeper jobs and reduces pop upkeep by 5%. The `planet_deep_sleep` modifier adds a further 233 jobs for a total of 333. [Definition](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/17_nomads_deposits.txt:62) · [Pod clearing](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/17_nomads_deposits.txt:55) | Arkship (colony ship) [Source](E:/SteamLibrary/steamapps/common/Stellaris/common/deposits/17_nomads_deposits.txt:56) |
